## Review round 16 — what a Worker sees of `$HOME`

**The decision** (a deliberate tightening of v6's "ro on the rest"; v6's "credential stores … out of reach" was unmet). `denySet` implemented "credential stores" as known names, and rounds 3, 14 and 15 each found one more: this machine alone has agents' tokens, app profiles, a wallet and shell histories outside it, and another project's `.env` under the owner's checkouts. For unattended sessions `$HOME` is now hidden whole and what is shown is listed (`landlock.ts › profile`): the task's own project checkout (`--reference`; v6), the running `node`, `HOME_READ`, and `policy.read`, the mirror of `policy.deny` (absolute paths; the deny set still wins inside one). hortusd's own `dist/` and `node_modules` stay `ro`; `~/.config/systemd` is `ro` for a chat and hidden here. Every other project's checkout is hidden wherever it lies, `/srv` or `/usr` included. A chat stays as v6 says.

- **`HOME_READ`, and why each entry:** `.local/bin`, `.local/lib` (pip's and installers' binaries and user site), `.local/share/pnpm` (pnpm's global bins, `pnpm link --global`), `.local/share/mise`, `.local/share/fnm`, `.nvm`, `.fnm`, `.volta`, `.asdf`, `.pyenv`, `.rbenv`, `.sdkman` (version managers), `.local/share/pipx`, `.local/share/uv/python`, `.local/share/uv/tools` (what `~/.local/bin` links to), `.rustup`, `.cargo/bin`, `go/bin`, `.bun`, `.deno`, `.dotnet`.
- **Stores inside those places, handled by naming less:** `~/.cargo` holds `credentials.toml`, so only `bin`; `~/.local/share/uv` holds `credentials/`, so only `python` and `tools`; `~/go` only `bin`. Left as a ceiling: an `etc/npmrc` under a `~/.nvm` node, if the owner ran `npm config set --global` with a token, and a script of the owner's in `~/.local/bin` with a key in it. Neither exists here (names only).
- **`~/.gitconfig` and `~/.config/git/config` are not shown.** Whether they hold credentials cannot be told in general (a token in a `url.insteadOf`, `http.extraHeader`), and a Worker needs nothing from them: identity, signing and credentials are forced through `GIT_CONFIG_*`. A shown one is worse: git dies on the `include` of a hidden file, and this owner's has an `includeIf`. What `git lfs install` wrote there is in `GIT_CONFIG_*` now.
- **An unattended session's `HOME` is its own** (`out/work/.cache/home`; `user.home` too). Not in the decision as written; measured necessary. A file a tool may not open is not a file that is missing: with the real `HOME`, every git command warned, `git config --global` was fatal, and libgit2, which honours no `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL`, killed `cargo new` and every git dependency (`'~/.gitconfig' is locked: Permission denied`, cargo 1.90). Only `HOME` reaches libgit2. It also closes yarn 1's `~/.npmrc` failure and makes `~/.foo` writes work. What finds itself by `$HOME` keeps its root: `RUSTUP_HOME`, `PYENV_ROOT`, `RBENV_ROOT`, `ASDF_DATA_DIR`, `VOLTA_HOME`, `MISE_DATA_DIR`, `PYTHONUSERBASE`. **Ceiling:** a toolchain outside that list that locates itself by `$HOME` is not found, loudly. Rustup was run; the others are from their documentation.
- **A symlink out of the list is refused**, as the target is what Landlock checks: `~/.local/bin/x → ~/.nvm/…` runs, `~/.local/bin/claude → ~/.local/share/claude/…` gets EACCES until `policy.read` names it. Following links would show what nobody listed.
- **A project at a linked worktree could not run at all** (major, a stuck task; `origin.ts › prepareClone`; older than this round): git refuses `--reference` to one. And a checkout with alternates would now leave a clone that cannot read them. `--reference` only where the objects are the checkout's own.
- **The installed unit has no PATH of the owner's** (major, normal use; `host.ts › sessionPath`; found reading `install.ts`): `cargo` in `~/.cargo/bin` was "not found" for every session under the system unit, tests never showing it as they inherit a shell's. A session's PATH adds node's directory and the usual bin directories. Not the unit's own: hortusd runs outside every sandbox, and a chat writes those directories. **Ceiling:** a version manager's bin with no fixed path (a second nvm node) is reached by its full path.
- **`policy.deny: ["~/x"]` denied nothing, silently** (major, a boundary): `deny`, `cache` and `read` must be absolute; a bad entry stops at validation with the key's name.
- **Chat**: the deny set gains `STORES`, one short list (agents, wallets, app profiles, histories) and `/run/libvirt` (the `libvirt` group is root, as `docker` is; this owner is in it). **Ceiling, said plainly:** a denylist cannot be complete, so a chat Worker's reach is the owner's, and the owner is watching. Other sockets stay reachable from a chat (the system bus, polkit).
- **VM Workers do not see `$HOME`:** `vm.ts › shares` is `ctl`, `out`, the DOF view, `repos`, a run's `schedule`, all under `HORTUS_HOME`; `HOME` is the guest's.

## Review round 16 — the other read grants

Through the real shim, unattended, on this machine. Scan: `find /var /etc /boot /srv /opt /usr/local /run -type f ! -perm -o=r -readable` (names only) for what the uid or its groups reach beyond the world.

| Path | Measured | Done |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `/proc/<pid>/environ`, `maps`, `mem`, `io`, `auxv`, `stack`, `cwd`, `root`, `exe`, `fd/<n>` of a process outside the domain | EACCES: Landlock's ptrace scoping | held |
| `/proc/<pid>/cmdline`, `status`, `mountinfo`, the numbers in `fd` | readable, the owner's and other sessions' | **ceiling.** No rule can name them and spare `/proc/self`, which a new pid's own directory needs; `hidepid` does not hide the same uid. Closing it takes a PID namespace and a private `/proc` in the shim, a subsystem. So a password in an owner's argv is readable. hortusd's own argv carries none (`repos.ts`) |
| `/proc/net/*`, `/proc/keys`, `/proc/kallsyms`, `boot_id` | metadata; own keys; zeroed addresses | nit |
| `/sys/firmware` (efivars readable) | one entry | hidden |
| `/sys/class/input`, `class/net`, `bus/usb`, `class/dmi/id`, `kernel/security` | names, MACs, device serials; DMI serials are root's | nit |
| `/sys/kernel/debug`, `tracing`, `fs/bpf`, `fs/pstore` | EACCES (root) | held |
| `/var/lib/systemd/coredump` | **readable** (the owner's ACL): the memory of whatever crashed | hidden |
| `/var/log` (`user-1000.journal` readable) | **readable** | hidden |
| `/var/lib/libvirt` (`libvirt` group) | **readable** | hidden |
| `/var/tmp`, `/var/mail`, `/var/spool` | the owner's | hidden |
| `/tmp` | **read-only since round 14, and full of what the owner's programs leave** | hidden (`policy.cache` gives it back) |
| `/mnt`, `/media`, `/run/media`, `/srv` | **the owner's disks**: `/mnt` has four here | hidden; a project there is shown, the rest is `policy.read` |
| other users' homes | DAC, and `/home` hidden | hidden |
| `/run/systemd`, `/run/dbus`, `/run/NetworkManager`, `/run/lock` | listable; `ro` grants no connect | nit; kept (`resolv.conf` may live there) |
| `/opt`, `/boot`, `/etc`, `/usr` | world-readable programs and config; toolchains live there (`/opt/rocm`) | kept |
| `/root`, `/etc/NetworkManager/system-connections`, `/var/lib/docker` | EACCES (root) | held |

The scan found nothing else: outside the rows above, the uid reaches only `/run/faillock/<owner>`. A walked directory cannot be listed (`/var/lib`, `/run`), which no toolchain here missed.

## Review round 16 — chat in real use

Real sandbox, chat profile, stdin `/dev/null` as `spawnSandboxed` has it. `script`: `failed to create pseudo-terminal: Permission denied`. `python -c 'import pty'`: imports, `spawn` and `openpty` raise `PermissionError`. `expect` is not installed here; it takes the same `openpty`. `npm init -y` works, bare `npm init` ends at EOF with status 1. `git rebase -i` works with `GIT_SEQUENCE_EDITOR`. `sudo` fails at once on `no new privileges`. The prompt now says all this in one line (no terminal, no root, use the non-interactive form).

- **`git rebase -i` and friends hung for good on the owner's editor** (major, a stuck task; `sandboxEnv`): this owner's `core.editor` is `nvim`, which waits forever with no terminal, and core's bash tool has no timeout. `GIT_EDITOR=true`, both kinds: the default todo or message is taken.
- **A chat's Maven failed where `~/.m2/repository` did not exist yet** (major, normal use; `sandboxEnv`): with a denied entry inside it, `~/.m2` is granted entry by entry, so nothing new can be made there: `Could not create local repository`. The task's own repository then. With one there, `mvn test` with a JUnit `@TempDir` finishes (`BUILD SUCCESS`): round 15's open point. The same holds of any walked directory: **ceiling**, v6's "an entry created later is unreachable"; a chat cannot make `~/x` or `~/.config/new`, and the prompt says a refused write is the sandbox.

## Review round 16 — second visits

Each stateful thing visited twice, by reading and, where it was not obvious, by a test; 15 fixes, each reverted once to see its test fail.

- **A self-hosted forge added in Settings stayed unknown to the proxy until a restart** (major; `vault/dof.ts › transformsDofModule`, `transforms.ts › expand`): hosts were expanded only at load. `reload: load`.
- **Rotating a key through the form dropped its `hosts` and `transform`** (major; `vault/index.ts › credential.set`), so model calls could fail. Kept unless sent.
- **With a dead site session `browser_login` answered "granted" forever** (major; `login-view.ts › browser.login`), and the owner was never asked. Asking again after cookies went out opens a login entry.
- **A rolling session's `Max-Age` was dated from the first capture, and a known value was scrubbed before capture** (major; `pipeline.ts › store`, `respond`): the jar dropped a live cookie and the task was logged out at its next start.
- **Two pending versions of one transform: approving both rolled back** (major, by the definitions: a wrong result the owner cannot see coming; `submit.ts › submit`). The list is newest first, so working down it ended on the older version, and its diff was against the enabled one. The fork had it as a nit. Now one open entry per transform, as for merges: a newer version replaces the pending one, and another task that waited on it is told.
- **`Save and reload` said nothing of what needs a restart** (minor: a misleading screen; `settings.ts`): `console.*` and `proxy` ports apply at the next start, and the form now says so.
- Minor: a live frame relay kept the handshake's scrubber after a rotation (`ws.ts`, `scrubber(p.host)` per frame); a GitHub App's cached token outlived a change or delete of its credential (`ops.ts`); `credential.delete` and `captured.forget` left grants alive; a run could schedule under a closed parent; `addRefs` left a ref with a closed task. Nit, fixed: expired minted tokens stayed in the scrub set; `#once#` keys are UUIDs.

**Held:** a transform updated or re-approved, a GitHub App token past expiry, a DOF renamed or re-added (sync state), a project renamed or moved (from the code), a chat reopened after a restart (by reading only), a second `task.close`, a webhook redelivered after processing, a Settings save that changes nothing (measured), a schedule edited during a live run but for the closed parent above. A rotated credential's scrubber held on the buffered path; the frame relay above was the break.

**`passkey.list`, `passkey.delete`** (a coordinator's decision; v6 "until the session is revoked" meant nothing for a lost device, as a thief who unlocks it signs in again). Settings › Remote lists passkeys beside sessions. Deleting one refuses its assertions and ends its sessions: `console_sessions.passkey` links them (a migration), and sessions from before it, which cannot be linked, end too, the safe side. An assertion in flight during the delete is refused. **The last passkey may be deleted**: the owner enrols again from loopback, and a remote console with none only shows the login page. Same role guard as `session.revoke`, so a stolen open session can delete keys too, v6's "is the owner until revoked".

Documented, not fixed:

- **A Discord token fixed after 4004 waits up to an hour** (minor, ceiling). The connector sleeps `fatalMs` (round 10: 1,440 IDENTIFYs a day would reset the token), and `credential.set` does not reach it; Sources keeps "check the bot token" meanwhile. Nothing is lost: reconcile heals it. A restart is the way past it. The fix needs a gateway restart per DOF on `credential.set`.
- **`browser_login` after cookies went out always opens a login.** hortusd cannot see a site's session end, so a Worker that asks twice for no reason costs the owner one needless entry: the safe side.
- **Clearing `hosts` or `transform`** on a credential is delete and add, which now also asks for its grant again.

Nits, not changed: a transform disabled by a hand edit answers "already enabled" to a resubmission of the same content; a concurrent second `task.close` writes its outcome over the first; a crash inside `finishClose`'s `rmSync` can publish an empty log over the real one on recovery (a millisecond window); `hortus chat -r` replays history without the owner's own lines; a removed DOF's rows stay in the owner's and Planner's search; WebAuthn's `InvalidStateError` for an already enrolled device shows as the browser's own text.

A follow-up in a thread was cut the same way. 2,000 now, all six connectors; the gateway cuts its log line and commit subject to 120. Ceiling: a longer paste is cut, and whitespace stays collapsed; 50 × 2,000 characters fits a batch in the context.
- **A session was offered `slack-app`** (major, wrong result; `tasks.ts › sessionCredentials`): the default scope was every vault name. - **`done` with a request waiting lost the reply** (major, wrong result; `prompt.ts`): after the merge landed, the Worker's Slack reply waited for approval, it said so with `send` and called `done`; the close expired the entry, and the owner never got the reply. A prompt line: end the turn without `done`. Measured 1 of 1 before, then 0 of 2 where the approval was held back. Ceiling: it is a prompt line, nothing refuses the `done`; the summary still says what was left open.
- **`hortus image build` could not work on a real home** (major, a v6 step unreachable; `build-image.sh`): `hortusd` writes `ca.crt` 0600, a plain `cp` as root kept that, and `runuser agent certutil` could not read it. Every image so far came from `/tmp/hortus-vm-home`, whose CA a test wrote by hand. `install -m 644`.
- **The two units.** `hortus install`: persistent, `User=`, `Environment=HOME=`, no `PATH` (systemd's), `Restart=on-failure`, `RestartPreventExitStatus=78`, `After=network-online.target`. `dev-run.sh`: transient (gone at a stop, so a `systemctl start` needs a unit file), `PATH=$PATH`, `HORTUS_HOME`, no restart, no `ExperimentalWarning` flag. - **Held, on the real unit:** git project and non-git project (`exclude`, shadow and origin at the merged sha, `status` clean) through clone, `npm test` on the unit's `PATH`, push, merge, approve; a chat in place, reopened after two restarts with its context; a `schedule` one-shot firing after a stop across its time; a stop mid-Worker and mid-VM: the merge had landed, the session was killed in 0.3 s, no session address, tap, rule or `qemu` left, and the task resumed and asked for its reply. `runtime: vm`: a live model in a guest (never run before), push, merge, resume. `ssh` under real nftables: `Connection timed out`, rc 255.

Through the real shim, unattended, fixture `HOME`, the unit's `PATH`.

- **A task's own installs were not on its `PATH`** (minor; `caches`): `go install` and `uv tool` succeeded and then gave "not found". The task's bin directories come first.

There is no size limit on `out/`, only the disk. Planner directories go by plain `rmSync`.

## Review round 17 — second visits, run

`src/e2e/visits.test.ts` (9 tests, default suite), on a real `HORTUS_NO_NET` daemon.

- **A chat's unread message at a restart** (major, stuck; `recovery.ts › recover`): a chat running at a stop or crash went to `waiting`, and an undrained owner message had nothing to wake it. `queued` when the inbox is above the cursor; with nothing unread it waits.
- **A moved checkout broke open clones** (major, stuck; `origin.ts › prepareClone`): alternates held the old path, and every git command failed. Rewritten at every start, with `replaceFile`: the fork's first `writeFileSync` followed a link the Worker could plant, so `hortusd` would have overwritten any file of the owner's (caught reading the merge; `projects.test.ts`). Ceiling: a move to a linked worktree leaves the clone broken, close the task; a session running during the move fails until its next start.
- **`project.delete` left open merges** (major, wrong result; `project.delete`): approved it died on `not our ref`, and after a re-add its `approved` status answered the task's next request. They are `replaced`, and the task is told.
- **A rename is a removal and an add** (ceiling): open tasks on the old name wait with `no project <old>`; the clone and the pushed branch in `<name>.git.removed-*` are kept, and adding the old name back resumes them. Moving tasks to a new name would be a new mechanism.
- **Held:** a chat reopened after a restart with its context and page; a chat mid-turn with nothing unread; a project directory gone (a forced sync refused, `main` untouched); a transform v1, v2, v1 across two tasks and a restart (one open entry, older ones `replaced`, the survivor installs). Nit: two tasks submitting identical content get two entries, and approving both installs the same thing.
